Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.cloneTree()

/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
2077 |   public Node cloneTree() {
2078 |     Node result = cloneNode();
2079 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
2080 |       Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();
2081 |       n2clone.parent = result;
2082 |       if (result.last != null) {
2083 |         result.last.next = n2clone;
2084 |       }
2085 |       if (result.first == null) {
2086 |         result.first = n2clone;
2087 |       }
2088 |       result.last = n2clone;
2089 |     }
2090 |     return result;
2091 |   }

===== M0005 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.ReferenceCollection.isWellDefined()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 310-333
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines if the variable for this reference collection is
     * "well-defined." A variable is well-defined if we can prove at
     * compile-time that it's assigned a value before it's used.
     *
     * Notice that if this function returns false, this doesn't imply that the
     * variable is used before it's assigned. It just means that we don't
     * have enough information to make a definitive judgement.
     */
Method code:
310 |     protected boolean isWellDefined() {
311 |       int size = references.size();
312 |       if (size == 0) {
313 |         return false;
314 |       }
315 | 
316 |       // If this is a declaration that does not instantiate the variable,
317 |       // it's not well-defined.
318 |       Reference init = getInitializingReference();
319 |       if (init == null) {
320 |         return false;
321 |       }
322 | 
323 |       Preconditions.checkState(references.get(0).isDeclaration());
324 |       BasicBlock initBlock = init.getBasicBlock();
325 |       for (int i = 1; i < size; i++) {
326 |         if (!initBlock.provablyExecutesBefore(
327 |                 references.get(i).getBasicBlock())) {
328 |           return false;
329 |         }
330 |       }
331 | 
332 |       return true;
333 |     }

===== M0006 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2923-2938
Documentation:
/**
   * Get the JSDocInfo for a function.
   */
Method code:
2923 |   public static JSDocInfo getFunctionJSDocInfo(Node n) {
2924 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
2925 |     JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
2926 |     if (fnInfo == null && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
2927 |       // Look for the info on other nodes.
2928 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
2929 |       if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
2930 |         // on ASSIGNs
2931 |         fnInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
2932 |       } else if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
2933 |         // on var NAME = function() { ... };
2934 |         fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
2935 |       }
2936 |     }
2937 |     return fnInfo;
2938 |   }

===== M0008 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SourceFile.getLine(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SourceFile.java
Original range: 205-250
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the source line for the indicated line number.
   *
   * @param lineNumber the line number, 1 being the first line of the file.
   * @return The line indicated. Does not include the newline at the end
   *     of the file. Returns {@code null} if it does not exist,
   *     or if there was an IO exception.
   */
Method code:
205 |   public String getLine(int lineNumber) {
206 |     String js = "";
207 |     try {
208 |       // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, this is optimized for few warnings.
209 |       // This is probably the right trade-off, but will be slow if there
210 |       // are lots of warnings in one file.
211 |       js = getCode();
212 |     } catch (IOException e) {
213 |       return null;
214 |     }
215 | 
216 |     int pos = 0;
217 |     int startLine = 1;
218 | 
219 |     // If we've saved a previous offset and it's for a line less than the
220 |     // one we're searching for, then start at that point.
221 |     if (lineNumber >= lastLine) {
222 |       pos = lastOffset;
223 |       startLine = lastLine;
224 |     }
225 | 
226 |     for (int n = startLine; n < lineNumber; n++) {
227 |       int nextpos = js.indexOf('\n', pos);
228 |       if (nextpos == -1) {
229 |         return null;
230 |       }
231 |       pos = nextpos + 1;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     // Remember this offset for the next search we do.
235 |     lastOffset = pos;
236 |     lastLine = lineNumber;
237 | 
238 |     if (js.indexOf('\n', pos) == -1) {
239 |       // If next new line cannot be found, there are two cases
240 |       // 1. pos already reaches the end of file, then null should be returned
241 |       // 2. otherwise, return the contents between pos and the end of file.
242 |       if (pos >= js.length()) {
243 |         return null;
244 |       } else {
245 |         return js.substring(pos, js.length());
246 |       }
247 |     } else {
248 |       return js.substring(pos, js.indexOf('\n', pos));
249 |     }
250 |   }

===== M0010 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineObjectLiterals.InliningBehavior.afterExitScope(NodeTraversal,ReferenceMap)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineObjectLiterals.java
Original range: 82-106
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |     @Override
83 |     public void afterExitScope(NodeTraversal t, ReferenceMap referenceMap) {
84 |       for (Iterator<Var> it = t.getScope().getVars(); it.hasNext();) {
85 |         Var v = it.next();
86 | 
87 |         if (isVarInlineForbidden(v)) {
88 |             continue;
89 |         }
90 | 
91 |         ReferenceCollection referenceInfo = referenceMap.getReferences(v);
92 | 
93 |         if (isInlinableObject(referenceInfo.references)) {
94 |             // Blacklist the object itself, as well as any other values
95 |             // that it refers to, since they will have been moved around.
96 |             staleVars.add(v);
97 | 
98 |             Reference declaration = referenceInfo.references.get(0);
99 |             Reference init = referenceInfo.getInitializingReference();
100 | 
101 |             // Split up the object into individual variables if the object
102 |             // is never referenced directly in full.
103 |             splitObject(v, declaration, init, referenceInfo);
104 |         }
105 |       }
106 |     }

Method code:
155 |   public void validateExpression(Node n) {
156 |     switch (n.getType()) {
157 |       // Childless expressions
158 |       case Token.FALSE:
159 |       case Token.NULL:
160 |       case Token.THIS:
161 |       case Token.TRUE:
162 |         validateChildless(n);
163 |         return;
164 | 
165 |       // General uniary ops
166 |       case Token.DELPROP:
167 |       case Token.POS:
168 |       case Token.NEG:
169 |       case Token.NOT:
170 |       case Token.INC:
171 |       case Token.DEC:
172 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
173 |       case Token.VOID:
174 |       case Token.BITNOT:
175 |         validateUnaryOp(n);
176 |         return;
177 | 
178 |       // General binary ops
179 |       case Token.COMMA:
180 |       case Token.OR:
181 |       case Token.AND:
182 |       case Token.BITOR:
183 |       case Token.BITXOR:
184 |       case Token.BITAND:
185 |       case Token.EQ:
186 |       case Token.NE:
187 |       case Token.SHEQ:
188 |       case Token.SHNE:
189 |       case Token.LT:
190 |       case Token.GT:
191 |       case Token.LE:
192 |       case Token.GE:
193 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
194 |       case Token.IN:
195 |       case Token.LSH:
196 |       case Token.RSH:
197 |       case Token.URSH:
198 |       case Token.SUB:
199 |       case Token.ADD:
200 |       case Token.MUL:
201 |       case Token.MOD:
202 |       case Token.DIV:
203 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
204 |         return;
205 | 
206 |       // Assignments
207 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
208 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
209 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
210 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
212 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
213 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
214 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
215 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
216 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
217 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
218 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
219 |         validateAssignmentExpression(n);
220 |         return;
221 | 
222 |       case Token.HOOK:
223 |         validateTrinaryOp(n);
224 |         return;
225 | 
226 |       // Node types that require special handling
227 |       case Token.STRING:
228 |         validateString(n);
229 |         return;
230 | 
231 |       case Token.NUMBER:
232 |         validateNumber(n);
233 |         return;
234 | 
235 |       case Token.NAME:
236 |         validateName(n);
237 |         return;
238 | 
239 |       case Token.GETELEM:
240 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
241 |         return;
242 | 
243 |       case Token.GETPROP:
244 |         validateGetProp(n);
245 |         return;
246 | 
247 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
248 |         validateArrayLit(n);
249 |         return;
250 | 
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         validateObjectLit(n);
253 |         return;
254 | 
255 |       case Token.REGEXP:
256 |         validateRegExpLit(n);
257 |         return;
258 | 
259 |       case Token.CALL:
260 |         validateCall(n);
261 |         return;
262 | 
263 |       case Token.NEW:
264 |         validateNew(n);
265 |         return;
266 | 
267 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
268 |         validateFunctionExpression(n);
269 |         return;
270 | 
271 |       default:
272 |         violation("Expected expression but was "
273 |             + Node.tokenToName(n.getType()), n);
274 |     }
275 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType)

Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
469 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
470 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
471 |          current != null;
472 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
473 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
474 |         return true;
475 |       }
476 |     }
477 |     return false;
478 |   }

===== M0043 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineObjectLiterals.InliningBehavior.computeVarList(Var,ReferenceCollection)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineObjectLiterals.java
Original range: 243-282
Documentation:
/**
     * Computes a list of ever-referenced keys in the object being
     * inlined, and returns a mapping of key name -> generated
     * variable name.
     */
Method code:
243 |     private Map<String, String> computeVarList(
244 |         Var v, ReferenceCollection referenceInfo) {
245 |       Map<String, String> varmap = Maps.newLinkedHashMap();
246 | 
247 |       for (Reference ref : referenceInfo.references) {
248 |         if (ref.isLvalue() || ref.isInitializingDeclaration()) {
249 |           Node val = ref.getAssignedValue();
250 |           if (val != null) {
251 |             Preconditions.checkState(val.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT);
252 |             for (Node child = val.getFirstChild(); child != null;
253 |                  child = child.getNext()) {
254 |               String varname = child.getString();
255 |               if (varmap.containsKey(varname)) {
256 |                 continue;
257 |               }
258 | 
259 |               String var = VAR_PREFIX + varname + "_" +
260 |                 safeNameIdSupplier.get();
261 |               varmap.put(varname, var);
262 |             }
263 |           }
264 |         } else if (ref.getParent().getType() == Token.VAR) {
265 |           // This is the var. There is no value.
266 |         } else {
267 |           Node getprop = ref.getParent();
268 |           Preconditions.checkState(getprop.getType() == Token.GETPROP);
269 | 
270 |           // The key being looked up in the original map.
271 |           String varname = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
272 |           if (varmap.containsKey(varname)) {
273 |             continue;
274 |           }
275 | 
276 |           String var = VAR_PREFIX + varname + "_" + safeNameIdSupplier.get();
277 |           varmap.put(varname, var);
278 |         }
279 |       }
280 | 
281 |       return varmap;
282 |     }

===== M0050 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.doStatementNormalizations(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 434-455
Documentation:
/**
     * Do normalizations that introduce new siblings or parents.
     */
Method code:
434 |     private void doStatementNormalizations(
435 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
436 |       if (n.getType() == Token.LABEL) {
437 |         normalizeLabels(n);
438 |       }
439 | 
440 |       // Only inspect the children of SCRIPTs, BLOCKs and LABELs, as all these
441 |       // are the only legal place for VARs and FOR statements.
442 |       if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n) || n.getType() == Token.LABEL) {
443 |         extractForInitializer(n, null, null);
444 |       }
445 | 
446 |       // Only inspect the children of SCRIPTs, BLOCKs, as all these
447 |       // are the only legal place for VARs.
448 |       if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
449 |         splitVarDeclarations(n);
450 |       }
451 | 
452 |       if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
453 |         moveNamedFunctions(n.getLastChild());
454 |       }
455 |     }

2801 |   static void visitPreOrder(Node node,

===== M0070 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 982-1009
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
982 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
983 |     // unknown
984 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
985 |       return true;
986 |     }
987 |     // equality
988 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
989 |       return true;
990 |     }
991 |     // all type
992 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
993 |       return true;
994 |     }
995 |     // unions
996 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
997 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
998 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
999 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1000 |           return true;
1001 |         }
1002 |       }
1003 |     }
1004 |     // named types
1005 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
1006 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
1007 |     }
1008 |     return false;
1009 |   }

===== M0073 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 99-160
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
99 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
100 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
101 |     // just bail out early here.
102 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
103 |       return this;
104 |     }
105 | 
106 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
107 | 
108 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
109 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
110 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
111 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
112 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
113 |     }
114 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
115 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
116 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
117 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
118 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
119 |         }
120 |       } else {
121 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
122 |           return this;
123 |         }
124 | 
125 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
126 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
127 |         // one another.
128 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
129 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
130 |           JSType current = it.next();
131 | 
132 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
133 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
134 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
135 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
136 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
137 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
138 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
139 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
140 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
141 |               return this;
142 |             }
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
145 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
146 |               return this;
147 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
148 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
149 |               it.remove();
150 |             }
151 |           }
152 |         }
153 |         alternates.add(alternate);
154 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
155 |       }
156 |     } else {
157 |       result = null;
158 |     }
159 |     return this;
160 |   }

===== M0095 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 167-188
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
167 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
168 |     if (isAllType) {
169 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
170 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
171 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
172 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
173 |       } else {
174 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
175 |       }
176 |     } else {
177 |       int size = alternates.size();
178 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
179 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
180 |       } else if (size > 1) {
181 |         return null;
182 |       } else if (size == 1) {
183 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
184 |       } else {
185 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
186 |       }
187 |     }
188 |   }

===== M0096 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 496-543
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
496 |     private void extractForInitializer(
497 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
498 | 
499 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
500 |         next = c.getNext();
501 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
502 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
503 |         switch (c.getType()) {
504 |           case Token.LABEL:
505 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
506 |             break;
507 |           case Token.FOR:
508 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
509 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
510 |               if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
511 |                 // Transform:
512 |                 //    for (var a = 1 in b) {}
513 |                 // to:
514 |                 //    var a = 1; for (a in b) {};
515 |                 Node newStatement = first;
516 |                 // Clone just the node, to remove any initialization.
517 |                 Node name = newStatement.getFirstChild().cloneNode();
518 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
519 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
520 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
521 |               }
522 |             } else if (c.getFirstChild().getType() != Token.EMPTY) {
523 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
524 |               Node empty = new Node(Token.EMPTY);
525 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
526 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
527 | 
528 |               Node newStatement;
529 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
530 |               // but are handled differently.
531 |               if (init.getType() == Token.VAR) {
532 |                 newStatement = init;
533 |               } else {
534 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
535 |               }
536 | 
537 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
538 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
539 |             }
540 |             break;
541 |         }
542 |       }
543 |     }

===== M0097 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ComposeWarningsGuard.GuardComparator.compare(WarningsGuard,WarningsGuard)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ComposeWarningsGuard.java

Documentation:

Method code:
60 |     @Override
61 |     public int compare(WarningsGuard a, WarningsGuard b) {

63 |       if (priorityDiff != 0) {
64 |         return priorityDiff;
65 |       }
66 |

68 |       // was added last wins.
69 |       return orderOfAddition.get(b).intValue() -
70 |           orderOfAddition.get(a).intValue();
71 |     }

456 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments)

466 |

===== M0130 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalVarReferenceMap.updateGlobalVarReferences(Map<Var, ReferenceCollection>,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalVarReferenceMap.java
Original range: 102-121
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the internal reference map based on the provided parameters. If
   * {@code scriptRoot} is not SCRIPT, it basically replaces the internal map
   * with the new one, otherwise it replaces all the information associated to
   * the given script.
   *
   * @param refMapPatch The reference map result of a
   *     {@link ReferenceCollectingCallback} pass which might be collected from
   *     the whole AST or just a sub-tree associated to a SCRIPT node.
   * @param root AST sub-tree root on which reference collection was done.
   */
Method code:
102 |   void updateGlobalVarReferences(Map<Var, ReferenceCollection>
103 |       refMapPatch, Node root) {
104 |     if (refMap == null || root.getType() != Token.SCRIPT) {
105 |       resetGlobalVarReferences(refMapPatch);
106 |       return;
107 |     }
108 | 
109 |     InputId inputId = root.getInputId();
110 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
111 |     // Note there are two assumptions here (i) the order of compiler inputs
112 |     // has not changed and (ii) all references are in the order they appear
113 |     // in AST (this is enforced in ReferenceCollectionCallback).
114 |     removeScriptReferences(inputId);
115 |     for (Entry<Var, ReferenceCollection> entry : refMapPatch.entrySet()) {
116 |       Var var = entry.getKey();
117 |       if (var.isGlobal()) {
118 |         replaceReferences(var.getName(), inputId, entry.getValue());
119 |       }
120 |     }
121 |   }

===== M0134 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 593-651
Documentation:

Method code:
593 |     @Override
594 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
595 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
596 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
597 |       if (name == null) {
598 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
599 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
600 |           errorReporter.error(
601 |             "unnamed function statement",
602 |             sourceName,
603 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
604 |         }
605 |         name = new Name();
606 |         name.setIdentifier("");
607 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
608 |       }
609 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
610 |       Node newName = transform(name);
611 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
612 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
613 |         // declaration.
614 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
615 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
616 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
617 |         // same line as the function keyword.
618 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
619 |             functionNode.getLp();
620 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
621 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
622 |       }
623 | 
624 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
625 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
626 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
627 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
628 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
629 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
630 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
631 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
632 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
633 |       if (fnName != null) {
634 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
635 |       } else {
636 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
637 |       }
638 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
639 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
640 | 
641 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
642 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
643 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
644 |       }
645 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
646 | 
647 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
648 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
649 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
650 |      return node;
651 |     }

===== M0139 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1332-1340
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1332 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1333 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1334 |       return -1;
1335 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1336 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1337 |     } else {
1338 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1339 |     }
1340 |   }

===== M0140 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 171-202
Documentation:

Method code:
171 |     @Override
172 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
173 |       // Note: Constant properties annotations are not propagated.
174 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
175 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
176 |           return;
177 |         }
178 | 
179 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
180 |         // Find the JSDocInfo for a top level variable.
181 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
182 |         if (var != null) {
183 |           info = var.getJSDocInfo();
184 |         }
185 | 
186 |         boolean shouldBeConstant =
187 |             (info != null && info.isConstant()) ||
188 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
189 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent);
190 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
191 |         if (shouldBeConstant && !isMarkedConstant) {
192 |           if (assertOnChange) {
193 |             String name = n.getString();
194 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
195 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
196 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
197 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
198 |           }
199 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
200 |         }
201 |       }
202 |     }

50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,

55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),

===== M0193 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2513-2523
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2513 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2514 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2515 |     int len = s.length();
2516 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2517 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2518 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2519 |         return false;
2520 |       }
2521 |     }
2522 |     return true;
2523 |   }

===== M0198 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 270-293
Documentation:

Method code:
270 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
271 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
272 |       return;
273 |     }
274 | 
275 |     try {
276 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
277 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
278 | 
279 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
280 |       sourceName = "";
281 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
282 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
283 | 
284 |       for (Node root : roots) {
285 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
286 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
287 |       }
288 | 
289 |       popScope();
290 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
291 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
292 |     }
293 |   }

===== M0201 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.moveNamedFunctions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 577-609
Documentation:
/**
     * Move all the functions that are valid at the execution of the first
     * statement of the function to the beginning of the function definition.
     */
Method code:
577 |     private void moveNamedFunctions(Node functionBody) {
578 |       Preconditions.checkState(
579 |           functionBody.getParent().getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
580 |       Node previous = null;
581 |       Node current = functionBody.getFirstChild();
582 |       // Skip any declarations at the beginning of the function body, they
583 |       // are already in the right place.
584 |       while (current != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
585 |         previous = current;
586 |         current = current.getNext();
587 |       }
588 | 
589 |       // Find any remaining declarations and move them.
590 |       Node insertAfter = previous;
591 |       while (current != null) {
592 |         // Save off the next node as the current node maybe removed.
593 |         Node next = current.getNext();
594 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
595 |           // Remove the declaration from the body.
596 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(previous);
597 |           functionBody.removeChildAfter(previous);
598 | 
599 |           // Readd the function at the top of the function body (after any
600 |           // previous declarations).
601 |           insertAfter = addToFront(functionBody, current, insertAfter);
602 |           reportCodeChange("Move function declaration not at top of function");
603 |         } else {
604 |           // Update the previous only if the current node hasn't been moved.
605 |           previous = current;
606 |         }
607 |         current = next;
608 |       }
609 |     }

===== M0204 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 60-77
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
60 |   @Override
61 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
62 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
63 |       return false;
64 |     }
65 | 
66 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
67 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
68 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
69 |     if (pos >= 0) {
70 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
71 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
72 |         return false;
73 |       }
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     return isConstantKey(name);
77 |   }

Original range: 382-385

===== M0214 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)

/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;

===== M0218 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 328-355
Documentation:

Method code:
328 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
329 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
330 |     // two cases:
331 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
332 |     // and
333 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
334 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
335 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
336 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
337 |     //
338 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
339 |     //
340 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
341 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
342 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
343 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
344 |     //
345 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
346 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
347 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
348 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType() ||
349 |         !(baseType instanceof PrototypeObjectType)) {
350 | 
351 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
352 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
353 |     }
354 |     setPrototype((PrototypeObjectType) baseType, propertyNode);
355 |   }

===== M0219 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 489-509
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
489 |   @Override
490 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
491 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
492 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
493 |     if (unknown) {
494 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
495 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
496 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
497 |         unknown = false;
498 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
499 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
500 |             unknown = true;
501 |             break;
502 |           }
503 |         }
504 |       } else {
505 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
506 |       }
507 |     }
508 |     return unknown;
509 |   }

===== M0235 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineObjectLiterals.InliningBehavior.replaceAssignmentExpression(Var,Reference,Map<String, String>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineObjectLiterals.java
Original range: 303-360
Documentation:
/**
     * Replaces an assignment like x = {...} with t1=a,t2=b,t3=c,true.
     * Note that the resulting expression will always evaluate to
     * true, as would the x = {...} expression.
     */
Method code:
303 |     private void replaceAssignmentExpression(Var v, Reference ref,
304 |                                              Map<String, String> varmap) {
305 |       // Compute all of the assignments necessary
306 |       List<Node> nodes = Lists.newArrayList();
307 |       Node val = ref.getAssignedValue();
308 |       blacklistVarReferencesInTree(val, v.scope);
309 |       Preconditions.checkState(val.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT);
310 |       Set<String> all = Sets.newLinkedHashSet(varmap.keySet());
311 |       for (Node key = val.getFirstChild(); key != null;
312 |            key = key.getNext()) {
313 |         String var = key.getString();
314 |         Node value = key.removeFirstChild();
315 |         // TODO(user): Copy type information.
316 |         nodes.add(
317 |           new Node(Token.ASSIGN,
318 |                    Node.newString(Token.NAME, varmap.get(var)), value));
319 |         all.remove(var);
320 |       }
321 | 
322 |       // TODO(user): Better source information.
323 |       for (String var : all) {
324 |         nodes.add(
325 |           new Node(Token.ASSIGN,
326 |                    Node.newString(Token.NAME, varmap.get(var)),
327 |                    NodeUtil.newUndefinedNode(null)));
328 |       }
329 | 
330 |       Node replacement;
331 |         // All assignments evaluate to true, so make sure that the
332 |         // expr statement evaluates to true in case it matters.
333 |         nodes.add(new Node(Token.TRUE));
334 | 
335 |         // Join these using COMMA.  A COMMA node must have 2 children, so we
336 |         // create a tree. In the tree the first child be the COMMA to match
337 |         // the parser, otherwise tree equality tests fail.
338 |         nodes = Lists.reverse(nodes);
339 |         replacement = new Node(Token.COMMA);
340 |         Node cur = replacement;
341 |         int i;
342 |         for (i = 0; i < nodes.size() - 2; i++) {
343 |           cur.addChildToFront(nodes.get(i));
344 |           Node t = new Node(Token.COMMA);
345 |           cur.addChildToFront(t);
346 |           cur = t;
347 |         }
348 |         cur.addChildToFront(nodes.get(i));
349 |         cur.addChildToFront(nodes.get(i + 1));
350 | 
351 |       Node replace = ref.getParent();
352 |       replacement.copyInformationFromForTree(replace);
353 | 
354 |       if (replace.getType() == Token.VAR) {
355 |         replace.getParent().replaceChild(
356 |             replace, NodeUtil.newExpr(replacement));
357 |       } else {
358 |         replace.getParent().replaceChild(replace, replacement);
359 |       }
360 |     }

===== M0243 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0261 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 452-481
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
452 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
453 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
454 |     int type = n.getType();
455 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
456 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
457 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
458 |     }
459 | 
460 |     curNode = n;
461 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
462 | 
463 |     switch (type) {
464 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
465 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
466 |         break;
467 | 
468 |       default:
469 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
470 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
471 |           // would no longer point to the true next
472 |           Node next = child.getNext();
473 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
474 |           child = next;
475 |         }
476 |         break;
477 |     }
478 | 
479 |     curNode = n;
480 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
481 |   }

===== M0271 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 441-489
Documentation:

Method code:
441 |   @Override
442 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
443 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
444 |       return true;
445 |     }
446 | 
447 |     // Union types
448 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
449 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
450 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
451 |       return false;
452 |     }
453 | 
454 |     // record types
455 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
456 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
457 |     }
458 | 
459 |     // Interfaces
460 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
461 |     // to the interface instance.
462 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
463 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
464 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
465 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
466 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
467 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
468 |           return true;
469 |         }
470 |       }
471 |     }
472 | 
473 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
474 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
475 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
476 |           return true;
477 |         }
478 |       }
479 |     }
480 | 
481 |     // other prototype based objects
482 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
483 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
484 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
485 |       // to avoid guessing.
486 |       return true;
487 |     }
488 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
489 |   }

463 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
464 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {

===== M0274 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 86-735
Documentation:

Method code:
86 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
87 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
88 |       return;
89 |     }
90 | 
91 |     int type = n.getType();
92 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
93 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
94 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
95 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
96 | 
97 |     // Handle all binary operators
98 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
99 |       Preconditions.checkState(
100 |           childCount == 2,
101 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
102 |           opstr, childCount);
103 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
104 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
105 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
106 | 
107 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
108 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
109 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
110 | 
111 |       // Handle associativity.
112 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
113 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
114 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
115 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
116 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
117 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
118 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
119 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
120 |       } else {
121 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
122 |       }
123 |       return;
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
127 | 
128 |     switch (type) {
129 |       case Token.TRY: {
130 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
131 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
132 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
133 | 
134 |         add("try");
135 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
136 | 
137 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
138 |         // isn't a catch block
139 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
140 |         if (catchblock != null) {
141 |           add(catchblock);
142 |         }
143 | 
144 |         if (childCount == 3) {
145 |           add("finally");
146 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
147 |         }
148 |         break;
149 |       }
150 | 
151 |       case Token.CATCH:
152 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
153 |         add("catch(");
154 |         add(first);
155 |         add(")");
156 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
157 |         break;
158 | 
159 |       case Token.THROW:
160 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
161 |         add("throw");
162 |         add(first);
163 | 
164 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
165 |         // parse this.
166 |         cc.endStatement(true);
167 |         break;
168 | 
169 |       case Token.RETURN:
170 |         add("return");
171 |         if (childCount == 1) {
172 |           add(first);
173 |         } else {
174 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
175 |         }
176 |         cc.endStatement();
177 |         break;
178 | 
179 |       case Token.VAR:
180 |         if (first != null) {
181 |           add("var ");
182 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
183 |         }
184 |         break;
185 | 
186 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
187 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
188 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
189 |         break;
190 | 
191 |       case Token.NAME:
192 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
193 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
194 |         } else {
195 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
196 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
197 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
198 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
199 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
200 |           } else {
201 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
202 |             // precedence.
203 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
204 |           }
205 |         }
206 |         break;
207 | 
208 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
209 |         add("[");
210 |         addArrayList(first);
211 |         add("]");
212 |         break;
213 | 
214 |       case Token.LP:
215 |         add("(");
216 |         addList(first);
217 |         add(")");
218 |         break;
219 | 
220 |       case Token.COMMA:
221 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
222 |         addList(first, false, context);
223 |         break;
224 | 
225 |       case Token.NUMBER:
226 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
227 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
228 |         break;
229 | 
230 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
231 |       case Token.VOID:
232 |       case Token.NOT:
233 |       case Token.BITNOT:
234 |       case Token.POS: {
235 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
236 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
237 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
238 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
239 |         break;
240 |       }
241 | 
242 |       case Token.NEG: {
243 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
244 | 
245 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
246 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
247 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
248 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
249 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
250 |         } else {
251 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
252 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
253 |         }
254 | 
255 |         break;
256 |       }
257 | 
258 |       case Token.HOOK: {
259 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
260 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
261 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
262 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
263 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
264 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
265 |         addExpr(last, 1);
266 |         break;
267 |       }
268 | 
269 |       case Token.REGEXP:
270 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
271 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
272 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
273 |         }
274 | 
275 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
276 | 
277 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
278 |         if (childCount == 2) {
279 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
280 |         } else {
281 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
282 |           add(regexp);
283 |         }
284 |         break;
285 | 
286 |       case Token.GET_REF:
287 |         add(first);
288 |         break;
289 | 
290 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
291 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
292 |         add(first);
293 |         add(".");
294 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
295 |         break;
296 | 
297 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
298 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
299 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
300 |         }
301 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
302 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
303 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
304 |           add("(");
305 |         }
306 | 
307 |         add("function");
308 |         add(first);
309 | 
310 |         add(first.getNext());
311 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
312 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
313 | 
314 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
315 |           add(")");
316 |         }
317 |         break;
318 | 
319 |       case Token.GET:
320 |       case Token.SET:
321 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT);
322 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
323 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
324 | 
325 |         // Get methods are unnamed
326 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
327 |         if (type == Token.GET) {
328 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
329 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
330 |           add("get ");
331 |         } else {
332 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
333 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
334 |           add("set ");
335 |         }
336 | 
337 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
338 |         String name = n.getString();
339 |         Node fn = first;
340 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
341 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
342 | 
343 |         // Add the property name.
344 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
345 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
346 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
347 |             // unicode escaped.
348 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
349 |           add(name);
350 |         } else {
351 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
352 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
353 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
354 |             cc.addNumber(d);
355 |           } else {
356 |             addJsString(n.getString());
357 |           }
358 |         }
359 | 
360 |         add(parameters);
361 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
362 |         break;
363 | 
364 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
365 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
366 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
367 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
368 |         }
369 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
370 |         if (preserveBlock) {
371 |           cc.beginBlock();
372 |         }
373 | 
374 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
375 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
376 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
377 |                 !preserveBlock &&
378 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
379 |                 n.getParent().getType() == Token.SCRIPT);
380 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
381 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
382 | 
383 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
384 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
385 |             cc.endStatement();
386 |           }
387 | 
388 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
389 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
390 |           }
391 | 
392 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
393 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
394 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
395 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
396 |           }
397 |         }
398 |         if (preserveBlock) {
399 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
400 |         }
401 |         break;
402 |       }
403 | 
404 |       case Token.FOR:
405 |         if (childCount == 4) {
406 |           add("for(");
407 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
408 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
409 |           } else {
410 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
411 |           }
412 |           add(";");
413 |           add(first.getNext());
414 |           add(";");
415 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
416 |           add(")");
417 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
418 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
419 |         } else {
420 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
421 |           add("for(");
422 |           add(first);
423 |           add("in");
424 |           add(first.getNext());
425 |           add(")");
426 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
427 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
428 |         }
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.DO:
432 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
433 |         add("do");
434 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
435 |         add("while(");
436 |         add(last);
437 |         add(")");
438 |         cc.endStatement();
439 |         break;
440 | 
441 |       case Token.WHILE:
442 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
443 |         add("while(");
444 |         add(first);
445 |         add(")");
446 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
447 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
448 |         break;
449 | 
450 |       case Token.EMPTY:
451 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
452 |         break;
453 | 
454 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
455 |         Preconditions.checkState(
456 |             childCount == 2,
457 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
458 |         Preconditions.checkState(
459 |             last.getType() == Token.STRING,
460 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
461 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
462 |         if (needsParens) {
463 |           add("(");
464 |         }
465 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
466 |         if (needsParens) {
467 |           add(")");
468 |         }
469 |         add(".");
470 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
471 |         break;
472 |       }
473 | 
474 |       case Token.GETELEM:
475 |         Preconditions.checkState(
476 |             childCount == 2,
477 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
478 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
479 |         add("[");
480 |         add(first.getNext());
481 |         add("]");
482 |         break;
483 | 
484 |       case Token.WITH:
485 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
486 |         add("with(");
487 |         add(first);
488 |         add(")");
489 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
490 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       case Token.INC:
494 |       case Token.DEC: {
495 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
496 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
497 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
498 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
499 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
500 |         if (postProp != 0) {
501 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
502 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
503 |         } else {
504 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
505 |           add(first);
506 |         }
507 |         break;
508 |       }
509 | 
510 |       case Token.CALL:
511 |         // We have two special cases here:
512 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
513 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
514 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
515 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
516 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
517 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
518 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
519 |         // that must be preserved.
520 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
521 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
522 |           add("(0,");
523 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
524 |           add(")");
525 |         } else {
526 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
527 |         }
528 |         add("(");
529 |         addList(first.getNext());
530 |         add(")");
531 |         break;
532 | 
533 |       case Token.IF:
534 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
535 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
536 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
537 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
538 |           cc.beginBlock();
539 |         }
540 | 
541 |         add("if(");
542 |         add(first);
543 |         add(")");
544 | 
545 |         if (hasElse) {
546 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
547 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
548 |           add("else");
549 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
550 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
551 |         } else {
552 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
553 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
554 |         }
555 | 
556 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
557 |           cc.endBlock();
558 |         }
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.NULL:
562 |       case Token.THIS:
563 |       case Token.FALSE:
564 |       case Token.TRUE:
565 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
566 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
567 |         break;
568 | 
569 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
570 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
571 |         add("continue");
572 |         if (childCount == 1) {
573 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
574 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
575 |           }
576 |           add(" ");
577 |           add(first);
578 |         }
579 |         cc.endStatement();
580 |         break;
581 | 
582 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
583 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
584 |         add("debugger");
585 |         cc.endStatement();
586 |         break;
587 | 
588 |       case Token.BREAK:
589 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
590 |         add("break");
591 |         if (childCount == 1) {
592 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
593 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
594 |           }
595 |           add(" ");
596 |           add(first);
597 |         }
598 |         cc.endStatement();
599 |         break;
600 | 
601 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
602 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
603 | 
604 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
605 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
606 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
607 |         cc.endStatement();
608 |         break;
609 | 
610 |       case Token.NEW:
611 |         add("new ");
612 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
613 | 
614 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
615 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
616 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
617 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(first, Token.CALL, new MatchNotFunction())) {
618 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
619 |         }
620 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
621 | 
622 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
623 |         Node next = first.getNext();
624 |         if (next != null) {
625 |           add("(");
626 |           addList(next);
627 |           add(")");
628 |         }
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       case Token.STRING:
632 |         if (childCount !=
633 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
634 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0)) {
635 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
636 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
637 |         }
638 |         addJsString(n.getString());
639 |         break;
640 | 
641 |       case Token.DELPROP:
642 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
643 |         add("delete ");
644 |         add(first);
645 |         break;
646 | 
647 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
648 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
649 |         if (needsParens) {
650 |           add("(");
651 |         }
652 |         add("{");
653 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
654 |           if (c != first) {
655 |             cc.listSeparator();
656 |           }
657 | 
658 |           if (c.getType() == Token.GET || c.getType() == Token.SET) {
659 |             add(c);
660 |           } else {
661 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.getType() == Token.STRING);
662 |             String key = c.getString();
663 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
664 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
665 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
666 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
667 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
668 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
669 |                 // were unicode escaped.
670 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
671 |               add(key);
672 |             } else {
673 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
674 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
675 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
676 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
677 |               } else {
678 |                 addExpr(c, 1);
679 |               }
680 |             }
681 |             add(":");
682 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
683 |           }
684 |         }
685 |         add("}");
686 |         if (needsParens) {
687 |           add(")");
688 |         }
689 |         break;
690 |       }
691 | 
692 |       case Token.SWITCH:
693 |         add("switch(");
694 |         add(first);
695 |         add(")");
696 |         cc.beginBlock();
697 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
698 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
699 |         break;
700 | 
701 |       case Token.CASE:
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
703 |         add("case ");
704 |         add(first);
705 |         addCaseBody(last);
706 |         break;
707 | 
708 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
709 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
710 |         add("default");
711 |         addCaseBody(first);
712 |         break;
713 | 
714 |       case Token.LABEL:
715 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
716 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
717 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
718 |         }
719 |         add(first);
720 |         add(":");
721 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
722 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
723 |         break;
724 | 
725 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
726 |       // ignored for our purposes.
727 |       case Token.SETNAME:
728 |         break;
729 | 
730 |       default:
731 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
732 |     }
733 | 
734 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
735 |   }

===== M0277 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineObjectLiterals.InliningBehavior.blacklistVarReferencesInTree(Node,Scope)

Documentation:
/**
     * If there are any variable references in the given node tree,
     * blacklist them to prevent the pass from trying to inline the
     * variable. Any code modifications will have potentially made the
     * ReferenceCollection invalid.
     */
Method code:
114 |     private void blacklistVarReferencesInTree(Node root, final Scope scope) {
115 |       NodeUtil.visitPreOrder(root, new NodeUtil.Visitor() {
116 |         @Override
117 |         public void visit(Node node) {
118 |           if (node.getType() == Token.NAME) {
119 |             staleVars.add(scope.getVar(node.getString()));
120 |           }
121 |         }
122 |       }, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION);
123 |     }

===== M0280 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(PrototypeObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 362-405
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
362 |   boolean setPrototype(PrototypeObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
363 |     if (prototype == null) {
364 |       return false;
365 |     }
366 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
367 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
368 |       return false;
369 |     }
370 | 
371 |     PrototypeObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
372 |         ? null : (PrototypeObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
373 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
374 | 
375 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
376 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
377 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
378 | 
379 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
380 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
381 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
382 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
383 |     }
384 | 
385 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
386 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
387 |       if (superClass != null) {
388 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
389 |       }
390 | 
391 |       if (isInterface()) {
392 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
393 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
394 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
395 |           }
396 |         }
397 |       }
398 |     }
399 | 
400 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
401 |       clearCachedValues();
402 |     }
403 | 
404 |     return true;

===== M0281 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1594-1613
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1594 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1595 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1596 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1597 |     }
1598 | 
1599 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1600 |     Node n, n2;
1601 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1602 |          res == null && n != null;
1603 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1604 |       if (node2 == null) {
1605 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1606 |       }
1607 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1608 |       if (res != null) {
1609 |         return res;
1610 |       }
1611 |     }
1612 |     return res;
1613 |   }

105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.

===== M0298 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1988-1990
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1988 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1989 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1990 |   }

===== M0308 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.splitVarDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 553-571
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
553 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
554 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
555 |         next = c.getNext();
556 |         if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
557 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
558 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
559 |           }
560 | 
561 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
562 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
563 |             c.removeChild(name);
564 |             Node newVar = new Node(
565 |                 Token.VAR, name, n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
566 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
567 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
568 |           }
569 |         }
570 |       }
571 |     }

Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if a name node represents a constant variable.
   *
   * <p>Determining whether a variable is constant has three steps:
   * <ol>
   * <li>In CodingConventionAnnotator, any name that matches the
   *     {@link CodingConvention#isConstant(String)} is annotated with an
   *     IS_CONSTANT_NAME property.
   * <li>The normalize pass renames any variable with the IS_CONSTANT_NAME
   *     annotation and that is initialized to a constant value with
   *     a variable name inlucding $$constant.
   * <li>Return true here if the variable includes $$constant in its name.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param node A NAME or STRING node
   * @return True if the variable is constant
   */
Method code:
2882 |   static boolean isConstantName(Node node) {
2883 |     return node.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
2884 |   }

223 |   private void throwUnexpectedException(Exception unexpectedException) {

790 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
791 |     boolean valid = false;
