===== M0326 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.getType() == Token.LP);
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.getType() == Token.NAME);
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

===== M0332 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 64-96
Documentation:

Method code:
64 |   @Override
65 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
66 |     Node declarationRoot = t.getScopeRoot();
67 |     Renamer renamer;
68 |     if (nameStack.isEmpty()) {
69 |       // If the contextual renamer is being used the starting context can not
70 |       // be a function.
71 |       Preconditions.checkState(
72 |           declarationRoot.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
73 |           !(rootRenamer instanceof ContextualRenamer));
74 |       Preconditions.checkState(t.inGlobalScope());
75 |       renamer = rootRenamer;
76 |     } else {
77 |       renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
78 |     }
79 | 
80 |     if (declarationRoot.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
81 |       // Add the function parameters
82 |       Node fnParams = declarationRoot.getFirstChild().getNext();
83 |       for (Node c = fnParams.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
84 |         String name = c.getString();
85 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
86 |       }
87 | 
88 |       // Add the function body declarations
89 |       Node functionBody = declarationRoot.getLastChild();
90 |       findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
91 |     } else {
92 |       // Add the block declarations
93 |       findDeclaredNames(declarationRoot, null, renamer);
94 |     }
95 |     nameStack.push(renamer);
96 |   }

===== M0342 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineObjectLiterals.InliningBehavior.isVarInlineForbidden(Var)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineObjectLiterals.java
Original range: 128-144
Documentation:
/**
     * Whether the given variable is forbidden from being inlined.
     */
Method code:
128 |     private boolean isVarInlineForbidden(Var var) {
129 |       // A variable may not be inlined if:
130 |       // 1) The variable is defined in the externs
131 |       // 2) The variable is exported,
132 |       // 3) Don't inline the special RENAME_PROPERTY_FUNCTION_NAME
133 |       // 4) A reference to the variable has been inlined. We're downstream
134 |       //    of the mechanism that creates variable references, so we don't
135 |       //    have a good way to update the reference. Just punt on it.
136 | 
137 |       // Additionally, exclude global variables for now.
138 | 
139 |       return var.isGlobal()
140 |           || var.isExtern()
141 |           || compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(var.name)
142 |           || RenameProperties.RENAME_PROPERTY_FUNCTION_NAME.equals(var.name)
143 |           || staleVars.contains(var);
144 |     }

/**
   * For an assignment or variable declaration get the assigned value.
   * @return The value node representing the new value.
   */

1559 |   static Node getAssignedValue(Node n) {

===== M0352 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 486-521
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
486 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
487 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
488 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
489 | 
490 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
491 | 
492 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
493 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
494 | 
495 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
496 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
497 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
498 |     }
499 | 
500 |     curNode = n;
501 |     pushScope(n);
502 | 
503 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
504 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
505 |       // scope.
506 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
507 |     }
508 | 
509 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
510 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
511 | 
512 |     // Args
513 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
514 | 
515 |     // Body
516 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
517 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
518 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
519 | 
520 |     popScope();
521 |   }

===== M0353 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeLineBreak()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 433-453
Documentation:

Method code:
433 |     @Override
434 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
435 |       if (lineBreak) {
436 |         if (sawFunction) {
437 |           startNewLine();
438 |           sawFunction = false;
439 |         }
440 |       }
441 | 
442 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
443 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
444 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
445 |       int len = code.length();
446 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
447 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
448 |         if (ch == ';') {
449 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 |       maybeCutLine();
453 |     }

===== M0356 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.findDeclaredNames(Node,Node,Renamer)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 187-204
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
187 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
188 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
189 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
190 |     if (parent == null
191 |         || parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION
192 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
193 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
194 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());
195 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
196 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
197 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(nameNode.getString());
198 |       }
199 | 
200 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
201 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
202 |       }
203 |     }
204 |   }

===== M0365 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1154-1292
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1154 |   Node parseInputs() {
1155 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1156 | 
1157 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1158 |     // individual file parse trees.
1159 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1160 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1161 |     }
1162 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1163 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1164 |     }
1165 | 
1166 |     // Parse main js sources.
1167 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1168 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1169 | 
1170 |     externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1171 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1172 | 
1173 |     externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1174 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1175 | 
1176 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1177 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1178 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1179 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1180 |     }
1181 | 
1182 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1183 | 
1184 |     try {
1185 |       // Parse externs sources.
1186 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1187 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1188 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1189 |           return null;
1190 |         }
1191 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1192 |       }
1193 | 
1194 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1195 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1196 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1197 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1198 | 
1199 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1200 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1201 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1202 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1203 |           }
1204 |         }
1205 | 
1206 |         try {
1207 |           inputs =
1208 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1209 |               .manageDependencies(
1210 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1211 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1212 |           report(JSError.make(
1213 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1214 |           return null;
1215 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1216 |           report(JSError.make(
1217 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1218 |           return null;
1219 |         }
1220 |       }
1221 | 
1222 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1223 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1224 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1225 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1226 | 
1227 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1228 |         if (n == null) {
1229 |           continue;
1230 |         }
1231 | 
1232 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1233 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1234 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1235 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1236 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1237 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1238 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1239 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1240 | 
1241 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1242 | 
1243 |             externs.add(input);
1244 |             staleInputs = true;
1245 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1246 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1247 |             staleInputs = true;
1248 |           }
1249 |         }
1250 |       }
1251 | 
1252 |       if (staleInputs) {
1253 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1254 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1255 |       }
1256 | 
1257 |       // Build the AST.
1258 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1259 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1260 |         if (n == null) {
1261 |           continue;
1262 |         }
1263 | 
1264 |         if (devMode) {
1265 |           runSanityCheck();
1266 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1267 |             return null;
1268 |           }
1269 |         }
1270 | 
1271 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1272 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1273 | 
1274 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1275 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1276 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1277 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1278 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1279 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1280 |         }
1281 | 
1282 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1283 |       }
1284 | 
1285 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1286 |         return null;
1287 |       }
1288 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1289 |     } finally {
1290 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1291 |     }
1292 |   }

===== M0394 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 317-345
Documentation:

Method code:
317 |     @Override
318 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
319 |       switch (n.getType()) {
320 |         case Token.WHILE:
321 |           if (CONVERT_WHILE_TO_FOR) {
322 |             Node expr = n.getFirstChild();
323 |             n.setType(Token.FOR);
324 |             Node empty = new Node(Token.EMPTY);
325 |             empty.copyInformationFrom(n);
326 |             n.addChildBefore(empty, expr);
327 |             n.addChildAfter(empty.cloneNode(), expr);
328 |             reportCodeChange("WHILE node");
329 |           }
330 |           break;
331 | 
332 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
333 |           normalizeFunctionDeclaration(n);
334 |           break;
335 | 
336 |         case Token.NAME:
337 |         case Token.STRING:
338 |         case Token.GET:
339 |         case Token.SET:
340 |           if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalizedObfuscated()) {
341 |             annotateConstantsByConvention(n, parent);
342 |           }
343 |           break;

===== M0396 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.isBlockBoundary(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 207-237
Documentation:
/**
   * @return true if this node marks the start of a new basic block
   */
Method code:
207 |   private static boolean isBlockBoundary(Node n, Node parent) {
208 |     if (parent != null) {
209 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
210 |         case Token.DO:
211 |         case Token.FOR:
212 |         case Token.TRY:
213 |         case Token.WHILE:
214 |         case Token.WITH:
215 |           // NOTE: TRY has up to 3 child blocks:
216 |           // TRY
217 |           //   BLOCK
218 |           //   BLOCK
219 |           //     CATCH
220 |           //   BLOCK
221 |           // Note that there is an explcit CATCH token but no explicit
222 |           // FINALLY token. For simplicity, we consider each BLOCK
223 |           // a separate basic BLOCK.
224 |           return true;
225 |         case Token.AND:
226 |         case Token.HOOK:
227 |         case Token.IF:
228 |         case Token.OR:
229 |           // The first child of a conditional is not a boundary,
230 |           // but all the rest of the children are.
231 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
232 | 
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     return n.getType() == Token.CASE;
237 |   }

===== M0405 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-148
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
117 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
118 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
119 | 
120 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
121 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
123 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
127 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
128 |     }
129 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
130 | 
131 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
132 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
133 |     try {
134 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
135 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
136 |       logger.info(
137 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
138 |     } finally {
139 |       Context.exit();
140 |     }
141 |     Node root = null;
142 |     if (astRoot != null) {
143 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
144 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
145 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
146 |     }
147 |     return root;
148 |   }

===== M0411 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1077-1097
Documentation:

Method code:
1077 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1078 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1079 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1080 |       return s;
1081 |     }
1082 | 
1083 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1084 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1085 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1086 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1087 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1088 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1089 |       // crazy.
1090 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1091 |         sb.append(c);
1092 |       } else {
1093 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1094 |       }
1095 |     }
1096 |     return sb.toString();
1097 |   }

===== M0425 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineObjectLiterals.InliningBehavior.isInlinableObject(List<Reference>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineObjectLiterals.java
Original range: 154-228
Documentation:
/**
     * Counts the number of direct (full) references to an object.
     * Specifically we check for references of the following type:
     * <pre>
     *   x;
     *   x.fn();
     * </pre>
     */
Method code:
154 |     private boolean isInlinableObject(List<Reference> refs) {
155 |       boolean ret = false;
156 |       for (Reference ref : refs) {
157 |         Node name = ref.getNode();
158 |         Node parent = ref.getParent();
159 |         Node gramps = ref.getGrandparent();
160 | 
161 |         // Ignore indirect references, like x.y (except x.y(), since
162 |         // the function referenced by y might reference 'this').
163 |         //
164 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
165 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.getFirstChild() == name);
166 |           // A call target maybe using the object as a 'this' value.
167 |           if (gramps.getType() == Token.CALL
168 |               && gramps.getFirstChild() == parent) {
169 |             return false;
170 |           }
171 |           continue;
172 |         }
173 | 
174 |         // Only rewrite VAR declarations or simple assignment statements
175 |         if (!isVarOrAssignExprLhs(name)) {
176 |            return false;
177 |         }
178 | 
179 |         Node val = ref.getAssignedValue();
180 |         if (val == null) {
181 |           // A var with no assignment.
182 |           continue;
183 |         }
184 | 
185 |         // We're looking for object literal assignments only.
186 |         if (val.getType() != Token.OBJECTLIT) {
187 |           return false;
188 |         }
189 | 
190 |         // Make sure that the value is not self-refential. IOW,
191 |         // disallow things like x = {b: x.a}.
192 |         //
193 |         // TODO: Only exclude unorderable self-referential
194 |         // assignments. i.e. x = {a: x.b, b: x.a} is not orderable,
195 |         // but x = {a: 1, b: x.a} is.
196 |         //
197 |         // Also, ES5 getters/setters aren't handled by this pass.
198 |         for (Node child = val.getFirstChild(); child != null;
199 |              child = child.getNext()) {
200 |           if (child.getType() == Token.GET ||
201 |               child.getType() == Token.SET) {
202 |             // ES5 get/set not supported.
203 |             return false;
204 |           }
205 |           Node childVal = child.getFirstChild();
206 |           // Check if childVal is the parent of any of the passed in
207 |           // references, as that is how self-referential assignments
208 |           // will happen.
209 |           for (Reference t : refs) {
210 |             Node refNode = t.getParent();
211 |             while (!NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(refNode)) {
212 |               if (refNode == childVal) {
213 |                 // There's a self-referential assignment
214 |                 return false;
215 |               }
216 |               refNode = refNode.getParent();
217 |             }
218 |           }
219 |         }
220 | 
221 | 
222 |         // We have found an acceptable object literal assignment. As
223 |         // long as there are no other assignments that mess things up,
224 |         // we can inline.
225 |         ret = true;
226 |       }
227 |       return ret;
228 |     }

===== M0444 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 141-167
Documentation:

Method code:
141 |   @Override
142 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
143 |     switch (n.getType()) {
144 |       case Token.NAME:
145 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
146 |         if (newName != null) {
147 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
148 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
149 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the javadoc?
150 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
151 |           }
152 |           n.setString(newName);
153 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
154 |         }
155 |         break;
156 | 
157 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
158 |         // Remove function recursive name (if any).
159 |         nameStack.pop();
160 |         break;
161 | 
162 |       case Token.CATCH:
163 |         // Remove catch except name from the stack of names.
164 |         nameStack.pop();
165 |         break;
166 |     }

===== M0456 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0457 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1034-1147
Documentation:

Method code:
1034 |   private void toString(
1035 |       StringBuilder sb,
1036 |       boolean printSource,
1037 |       boolean printAnnotations,
1038 |       boolean printType) {
1039 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
1040 |       sb.append(Token.name(type));
1041 |       if (this instanceof StringNode) {
1042 |         sb.append(' ');
1043 |         sb.append(getString());
1044 |       } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
1045 |         sb.append(' ');
1046 |         // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
1047 |         // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
1048 |         // toStringTree.
1049 |         if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
1050 |           sb.append("<invalid>");
1051 |         } else {
1052 |           sb.append(first.getString());
1053 |         }
1054 |       } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
1055 |         ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode) this;
1056 |         if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
1057 |           FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode) this;
1058 |           sb.append(' ');
1059 |           sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
1060 |         }
1061 |         if (printSource) {
1062 |           sb.append(" [source name: ");
1063 |           sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
1064 |           sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
1065 |           sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd() - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
1066 |           sb.append("] [base line: ");
1067 |           sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
1068 |           sb.append("] [end line: ");
1069 |           sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
1070 |           sb.append(']');
1071 |         }
1072 |       } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
1073 |         sb.append(' ');
1074 |         sb.append(getDouble());
1075 |       }
1076 |       if (printSource) {
1077 |         int lineno = getLineno();
1078 |         if (lineno != -1) {
1079 |           sb.append(' ');
1080 |           sb.append(lineno);
1081 |         }
1082 |       }
1083 | 
1084 |       if (printAnnotations) {
1085 |         int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
1086 |         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
1087 |           int type = keys[i];
1088 |           PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
1089 |           sb.append(" [");
1090 |           sb.append(propToString(type));
1091 |           sb.append(": ");
1092 |           String value;
1093 |           switch (type) {
1094 |             case TARGETBLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it recurses
1095 |               value = "target block property";
1096 |               break;
1097 |             case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it is dull
1098 |               value = "last local block";
1099 |               break;
1100 |             case ISNUMBER_PROP:
1101 |               switch (x.getIntValue()) {
1102 |                 case BOTH:
1103 |                   value = "both";
1104 |                   break;
1105 |                 case RIGHT:
1106 |                   value = "right";
1107 |                   break;
1108 |                 case LEFT:
1109 |                   value = "left";
1110 |                   break;
1111 |                 default:
1112 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1113 |               }
1114 |               break;
1115 |             case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
1116 |               switch (x.getIntValue()) {
1117 |                 case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
1118 |                   value = "eval";
1119 |                   break;
1120 |                 case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
1121 |                   value = "with";
1122 |                   break;
1123 |                 default:
1124 |                   // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
1125 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1126 |               }
1127 |               break;
1128 |             default:
1129 |               value = x.toString();
1130 |               break;
1131 |           }
1132 |           sb.append(value);
1133 |           sb.append(']');
1134 |         }
1135 |       }
1136 | 
1137 |       if (printType) {
1138 |         if (jsType != null) {
1139 |           String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1140 |           if (jsTypeString != null) {
1141 |             sb.append(" : ");
1142 |             sb.append(jsTypeString);
1143 |           }
1144 |         }
1145 |       }
1146 |     }
1147 |   }

===== M0482 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 753-790
Documentation:

Method code:
753 |     @Override
754 |     Node processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral literalNode) {
755 |       if (literalNode.isDestructuring()) {
756 |         reportDestructuringAssign(literalNode);
757 |       }
758 | 
759 |       Node node = newNode(Token.OBJECTLIT);
760 |       for (ObjectProperty el : literalNode.getElements()) {
761 |         if (config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
762 |           if (el.isGetter()) {
763 |             reportGetter(el);
764 |             continue;
765 |           } else if (el.isSetter()) {
766 |             reportSetter(el);
767 |             continue;
768 |           }
769 |         }
770 | 
771 |         Node key = transformAsString(el.getLeft());
772 |         Node value = transform(el.getRight());
773 |         if (el.isGetter()) {
774 |           key.setType(Token.GET);
775 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
776 |           if (getFnParamNode(value).hasChildren()) {
777 |             reportGetterParam(el.getLeft());
778 |           }
779 |         } else if (el.isSetter()) {
780 |           key.setType(Token.SET);
781 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
782 |           if (!getFnParamNode(value).hasOneChild()) {
783 |             reportSetterParam(el.getLeft());
784 |           }
785 |         }
786 |         key.addChildToFront(value);
787 |         node.addChildToBack(key);
788 |       }
789 |       return node;
790 |     }

===== M0488 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1781-1844
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1781 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1782 |     if (type != node.getType()
1783 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1784 |         || getNodeClass(this) != getNodeClass(node)) {
1785 |       return false;
1786 |     }
1787 | 
1788 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1789 |       return false;
1790 |     }
1791 | 
1792 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1793 |       try {
1794 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1795 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1796 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1797 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1798 |             return false;
1799 |           }
1800 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1801 |           return false;
1802 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1803 |           return false;
1804 |         } else {
1805 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1806 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1807 |               return false;
1808 |             }
1809 |           }
1810 |         }
1811 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1812 |         return false;
1813 |       }
1814 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1815 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1816 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1817 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1818 |         return false;
1819 |       }
1820 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1821 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1822 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1823 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1824 |         return false;
1825 |       }
1826 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1827 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1828 |         return false;
1829 |       }
1830 |     }
1831 | 
1832 |     if (recurse) {
1833 |       Node n, n2;
1834 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1835 |            n != null;
1836 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1837 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1838 |           return false;
1839 |         }
1840 |       }
1841 |     }
1842 | 
1843 |     return true;
1844 |   }

===== M0502 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineObjectLiterals.InliningBehavior.splitObject(Var,Reference,Reference,ReferenceCollection)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineObjectLiterals.java
Original range: 366-437
Documentation:
/**
     * Splits up the object literal into individual variables, and
     * updates all uses.
     */
Method code:
366 |     private void splitObject(Var v, Reference declaration,
367 |                              Reference init,
368 |                              ReferenceCollection referenceInfo) {
369 |       // First figure out the FULL set of possible keys, so that they
370 |       // can all be properly set as necessary.
371 |       Map<String, String> varmap = computeVarList(v, referenceInfo);
372 | 
373 |       Map<String, Node> initvals = Maps.newHashMap();
374 |       // Figure out the top-level of the var assign node. If it's a plain
375 |       // ASSIGN, then there's an EXPR_STATEMENT above it, if it's a
376 |       // VAR then it should be directly replaced.
377 |       Node vnode;
378 |       boolean defined = referenceInfo.isWellDefined() &&
379 |           init.getParent().getType() == Token.VAR;
380 |       if (defined) {
381 |         vnode = init.getParent();
382 |         fillInitialValues(init, initvals);
383 |       } else {
384 |         // TODO(user): More test / rewrite this part.
385 |         // Find the beginning of the function / script.
386 |         vnode = v.getScope().getRootNode().getLastChild().getFirstChild();
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : varmap.entrySet()) {
390 |         Node val = initvals.get(entry.getKey());
391 |         Node varnode = NodeUtil.newVarNode(entry.getValue(), val);
392 |         if (val == null) {
393 |           // is this right?
394 |           varnode.copyInformationFromForTree(vnode);
395 |         } else {
396 |           blacklistVarReferencesInTree(val, v.scope);
397 |         }
398 |         vnode.getParent().addChildBefore(varnode, vnode);
399 |       }
400 | 
401 |       if (defined) {
402 |         vnode.getParent().removeChild(vnode);
403 |       }
404 | 
405 |       for (Reference ref : referenceInfo.references) {
406 |         // The init/decl have already been converted.
407 |         if (defined && ref == init) continue;
408 | 
409 |         if (ref.isLvalue()) {
410 |           // Assignments have to be handled specially, since they
411 |           // expand out into multiple assignments.
412 |           replaceAssignmentExpression(v, ref, varmap);
413 |         } else if (ref.getParent().getType() == Token.VAR) {
414 |           // The old variable declaration. It didn't have a
415 |           // value. Remove it entirely as it should now be unused.
416 |           ref.getGrandparent().removeChild(ref.getParent());
417 |         } else {
418 |           // Make sure that the reference is a GETPROP as we expect it to be.
419 |           Node getprop = ref.getParent();
420 |           Preconditions.checkState(getprop.getType() == Token.GETPROP);
421 | 
422 |           // The key being looked up in the original map.
423 |           String var = getprop.getChildAtIndex(1).getString();
424 | 
425 |           // If the variable hasn't already been declared, add an empty
426 |           // declaration near all the other declarations.
427 |           Preconditions.checkState(varmap.containsKey(var));
428 | 
429 |           // Replace the GETPROP node with a NAME.
430 |           Node replacement = Node.newString(Token.NAME, varmap.get(var));
431 |           replacement.copyInformationFrom(getprop);
432 |           ref.getGrandparent().replaceChild(ref.getParent(), replacement);
433 |         }
434 |       }
435 | 
436 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
437 |     }

===== M0505 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transformTree(AstRoot,StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 163-186
Documentation:

Method code:
163 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
164 |                                    StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
165 |                                    String sourceString,
166 |                                    Config config,
167 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
168 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, sourceFile,
169 |         config, errorReporter);
170 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
171 | 
172 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {
173 |       for (Comment comment : node.getComments()) {
174 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.JSDOC &&
175 |             !comment.isParsed()) {
176 |           irFactory.handlePossibleFileOverviewJsDoc(comment, irNode);
177 |         } else if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.BLOCK) {
178 |           irFactory.handleBlockComment(comment);
179 |         }
180 |       }
181 |     }
182 | 
183 |     irFactory.setFileOverviewJsDoc(irNode);
184 | 
185 |     return irNode;
186 |   }

Method code:
235 |   @Override
236 |   boolean defineProperty(String name, JSType type, boolean inferred,
237 |       Node propertyNode) {
238 |     if (hasOwnDeclaredProperty(name)) {
239 |       return false;
240 |     }
241 |     Property newProp = new Property(

243 |     Property oldProp = properties.get(name);

246 |       // replaceScript scenario.
247 |       newProp.setJSDocInfo(oldProp.getJSDocInfo());
248 |     }
249 |     properties.put(name, newProp);
250 |     return true;
251 |   }

/**

===== M0527 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateFunctions(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 234-256
Documentation:
/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
234 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
235 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(n);
236 | 
237 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
238 |       // which are var_args.
239 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
240 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
241 |            arg != null;
242 |            arg = arg.getNext()) {
243 |         String argName = arg.getString();
244 |         JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
245 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
246 | 
247 |         if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
248 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
249 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
250 |         }
251 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
252 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
253 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
254 |         }
255 |       }
256 |     }

56 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
57 |     if (checkOnly) {

59 |     } else {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 235-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

===== M0566 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 430-446
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
430 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
431 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
432 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
433 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
434 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
435 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
436 |         String inputName = input.getName();
437 | 
438 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
439 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
440 |         // will get an error down the line.
441 |         inputs.add(input);
442 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
443 |       }
444 |     }
445 |     return inputs;
446 |   }

===== M0597 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.BasicBlock.provablyExecutesBefore(BasicBlock)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 696-715
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether this block is guaranteed to begin executing before
     * the given block does.
     */
Method code:
696 |     boolean provablyExecutesBefore(BasicBlock thatBlock) {
697 |       // If thatBlock is a descendant of this block, and there are no hoisted
698 |       // blocks between them, then this block must start before thatBlock.
699 |       BasicBlock currentBlock;
700 |       for (currentBlock = thatBlock;
701 |            currentBlock != null && currentBlock != this;
702 |            currentBlock = currentBlock.getParent()) {
703 |         if (currentBlock.isHoisted) {
704 |           return false;
705 |         }
706 |       }
707 | 
708 |       if (currentBlock == this) {
709 |         return true;
710 |       }
711 |       if (isGlobalScopeBlock() && thatBlock.isGlobalScopeBlock()) {
712 |         return true;
713 |       }
714 |       return false;
715 |     }

Method code:
118 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
119 |     switch (node.getType()) {
120 |       case Token.ADD:
121 |       case Token.AND:
122 |       case Token.BITAND:
123 |       case Token.BITOR:
124 |       case Token.BITXOR:
125 |       case Token.COMMA:
126 |       case Token.DIV:
127 |       case Token.EQ:
128 |       case Token.GE:
129 |       case Token.GT:
130 |       case Token.IN:
131 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
132 |       case Token.LE:
133 |       case Token.LSH:
134 |       case Token.LT:
135 |       case Token.MOD:
136 |       case Token.MUL:
137 |       case Token.NE:
138 |       case Token.OR:
139 |       case Token.RSH:
140 |       case Token.SHEQ:
141 |       case Token.SHNE:
142 |       case Token.SUB:
143 |       case Token.URSH:
144 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
145 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
146 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
147 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
148 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
159 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
160 |       case Token.BITNOT:
161 |       case Token.DEC:
162 |       case Token.DELPROP:
163 |       case Token.INC:
164 |       case Token.NEG:
165 |       case Token.NOT:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
168 |       case Token.VOID:
169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:
186 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.FINALLY:
190 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
191 |       case Token.COLON:
192 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:
196 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
197 |       case Token.EMPTY:
198 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
199 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
200 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
201 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
202 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
203 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
204 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
205 |         } else {
206 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
207 |                                           node.getClass() +
208 |                                           " type: " +
209 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
210 |         }
211 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
212 |       case Token.FALSE:
213 |       case Token.NULL:
214 |       case Token.THIS:
215 |       case Token.TRUE:
216 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
217 |       case Token.FOR:
218 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
219 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
220 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
221 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
222 |         } else {
223 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
224 |                                           node.getClass() +
225 |                                           " type: " +
226 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
227 |         }
228 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
229 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
230 |       case Token.GETELEM:
231 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
232 |       case Token.GETPROP:
233 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
234 |       case Token.HOOK:
235 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
236 |       case Token.IF:
237 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
238 |       case Token.LABEL:
239 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
240 |       case Token.LP:
241 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
242 |       case Token.NAME:
243 |         return processName((Name) node);
244 |       case Token.NEW:
245 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
246 |       case Token.NUMBER:
247 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
248 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
249 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
250 |       case Token.REGEXP:
251 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
252 |       case Token.RETURN:
253 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
254 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
255 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
256 |       case Token.STRING:
257 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
258 |       case Token.SWITCH:
259 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
260 |       case Token.THROW:
261 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
262 |       case Token.TRY:
263 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
264 |       case Token.CONST:
265 |       case Token.VAR:
266 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
267 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
268 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
269 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
270 |         } else {
271 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
272 |                                           node.getClass() +
273 |                                           " type: " +
274 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
275 |         }
276 |       case Token.WHILE:
277 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
278 |       case Token.WITH:
279 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
280 |     }
281 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
282 |   }

88 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
89 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),

===== M0618 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 451-466
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
451 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
452 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
453 |       Set<String> directives = null;
454 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
455 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
456 |         if (directives == null) {
457 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
458 |         } else {
459 |           directives.add(directive);
460 |         }
461 |       }
462 | 
463 |       if (directives != null) {
464 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
465 |       }
466 |     }

/**
     * Mark names and properties that are constants by convention.
     */
Method code:
350 |     private void annotateConstantsByConvention(Node n, Node parent) {
351 |       Preconditions.checkState(
352 |           n.getType() == Token.NAME
353 |           || n.getType() == Token.STRING
354 |           || n.getType() == Token.GET
355 |           || n.getType() == Token.SET);
356 | 
357 |       // There are only two cases where a string token
358 |       // may be a variable reference: The right side of a GETPROP
359 |       // or an OBJECTLIT key.
360 |       boolean isObjLitKey = NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent);
361 |       boolean isProperty = isObjLitKey ||
362 |           (parent.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
363 |            parent.getLastChild() == n);
364 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME || isProperty) {
365 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
366 |         if (!isMarkedConstant &&
367 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
368 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent)) {
369 |           if (assertOnChange) {
370 |             String name = n.getString();
371 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
372 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
373 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
374 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
375 |           }
376 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
377 |         }
378 |       }
379 |     }

===== M0632 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.VerifyConstants.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 231-280
Documentation:

Method code:
231 |     @Override
232 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
233 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
234 |         String name = n.getString();
235 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
236 |           return;
237 |         }
238 | 
239 |         boolean isConst = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
240 |         if (checkUserDeclarations) {
241 |           boolean expectedConst = false;
242 |           CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
243 |           if (NodeUtil.isConstantName(n)
244 |               || NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(convention, n, parent)) {
245 |             expectedConst = true;
246 |           } else {
247 |             expectedConst = false;
248 | 
249 |             JSDocInfo info = null;
250 |             Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
251 |             if (var != null) {
252 |               info = var.getJSDocInfo();
253 |             }
254 | 
255 |             if (info != null && info.isConstant()) {
256 |               expectedConst = true;
257 |             } else {
258 |               expectedConst = false;
259 |             }
260 |           }
261 | 
262 |           if (expectedConst) {
263 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
264 |                 "The name " + name + " is not annotated as constant.");
265 |           } else {
266 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
267 |                 "The name " + name + " should not be annotated as constant.");
268 |           }
269 |         }
270 | 
271 |         Boolean value = constantMap.get(name);
272 |         if (value == null) {
273 |           constantMap.put(name, isConst);
274 |         } else {
275 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.booleanValue() == isConst,
276 |               "The name " + name + " is not consistently annotated as " +
277 |               "constant.");
278 |         }
279 |       }
280 |     }

===== M0634 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 118-149
Documentation:

Method code:
118 |   @Override
119 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
120 |     new NodeTraversal(
121 |         compiler, new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange))
122 |         .traverseRoots(externs, root);
123 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
124 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
125 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
126 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
127 |     }
128 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
129 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
130 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
131 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our currect Scope logic
132 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
133 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
134 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
135 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
136 |     //   function f() {
137 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
138 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
139 |     //   }
140 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
141 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a seperate scope for catch nodes.
142 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(externs, root);
143 |     new PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars(compiler, assertOnChange)
144 |         .process(externs, root);
145 | 
146 |     if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized()) {
147 |       compiler.setLifeCycleStage(LifeCycleStage.NORMALIZED);
148 |     }
149 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 228-291
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
228 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
229 |     this.options = options;
230 |     if (errorManager == null) {
231 |       if (outStream == null) {
232 |         setErrorManager(
233 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
234 |       } else {
235 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
236 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
237 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
238 |         setErrorManager(printer);
239 |       }
240 |     }
241 | 
242 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
243 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
244 |       options.checkTypes = true;
245 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
246 |       options.checkTypes = false;
247 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
248 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
249 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
250 |       // parser type warnings.
251 |       options.setWarningLevel(
252 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
253 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
254 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
258 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
259 |       options.setWarningLevel(
260 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
261 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
262 |     }
263 | 
264 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
265 |       options.setWarningLevel(
266 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
267 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
268 |     }
269 | 
270 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
271 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
272 |     guards.add(
273 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
274 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
275 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
276 | 
277 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
278 | 
279 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
280 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
281 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
282 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
283 |     // error off.
284 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
285 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
286 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
287 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
288 |     }
289 | 
290 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
291 |   }

===== M0643 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 616-628
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
616 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
617 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
618 |     if (typeSet == null) {
619 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
620 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
621 |     }
622 | 
623 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
624 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
625 | 
626 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
627 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
628 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isStatementParent(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1757-1770
Documentation:

Method code:
1757 |   static boolean isStatementParent(Node parent) {
1758 |     // It is not possible to determine definitely if a node is a statement
1759 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node can be
1760 |     // either part of an expression or a statement.
1761 |     Preconditions.checkState(parent != null);
1762 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
1763 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
1764 |       case Token.BLOCK:
1765 |       case Token.LABEL:
1766 |         return true;
1767 |       default:
1768 |         return false;
1769 |     }
1770 |   }
